Primary Request and Intent:\n   The user asked to run `compute_cka.py` on server westd-13176 using MVP 001 (Qwen2.5-7B) cached activations as a pilot to verify the code and produce CKA results. The task involves 5 steps: (1) find MVP cached activations, (2) understand compute_cka.py input requirements, (3) find probe metrics for correlation analysis, (4) run the pilot with n_bootstrap=1000, seed=42, and (5) check output and report CKA matrix, same-layer cross-language Spearman, partial Spearman controlling for depth, per-language Spearman, and any code bugs.\n\n2. Key Technical Concepts:\n   - Linear CKA (Kornblith et al., 2019): Centered Kernel Alignment between activation matrices\n   - Bootstrap confidence intervals (1000 resamples) for CKA values and correlation statistics\n   - Spearman rank correlation between CKA and transfer degradation\n   - Partial Spearman controlling for normalized depth\n   - Subspace angle analysis between EN and target language probe directions\n   - pt_flat activation format: `layer{N}_{lang}.pt` files containing torch tensors with 70/30 train/eval split\n   - Cross-lingual calibration probing with logistic regression probes\n   - Transfer degradation = en_auroc[layer] - zs_auroc[(layer, lang)]\n\n3. Files and Code Sections:\n   - `/root/xling_calib_direction/code/compute_cka.py`\n     - Main analysis script. Supports two activation formats: \"npy\" (L*/en_eval.npy) and \"pt_flat\" (layer*_en.pt)\n     - Key functions:\n       ```python\n       def linear_cka(X, Y):\n           X = X - X.mean(axis=0)\n           Y = Y - Y.mean(axis=0)\n           XTX = X.T @ X; YTY = Y.T @ Y; YTX = Y.T @ X\n           num = np.linalg.norm(YTX, 'fro') ** 2\n           den = np.linalg.norm(XTX, 'fro') * np.linalg.norm(YTY, 'fro')\n           return float(num / den) if den != 0 else 0.0\n       ```\n       ```python\n       def load_eval_activations(act_dir, fmt, layer, lang):\n           # pt_flat format: loads .pt, takes last 30% as eval\n           data = torch.load(path, weights_only=True)\n           acts = data[\"activations\"].numpy()\n           n = len(acts); split = int(n * 0.7)\n           return acts[split:]\n       ```\n       ```python\n       def load_metrics(metrics_dir, model_name):\n           # Fallback chain: new JSON format → old CSV format (train_metrics.csv + transfer_results.csv) → probes/train_metrics.csv\n       ```\n     - Argparse: `--activation_dir` (required), `--model_name` (required), `--metrics_dir` (default=\"results\"), `--probes_dir` (default=None, falls back to metrics_dir), `--output_dir`, `--n_bootstrap` (default=1000), `--seed` (default=42), `--languages`, `--layers`, `--skip_subspace`\n     - probes_dir defaults to metrics_dir (line 323-324: `if args.probes_dir is None: args.probes_dir = args.metrics_dir`)\n\n   - `/root/xling_calib_direction/activations/` — 48 .pt files, ~200MB each\n     - pt_flat format: layer{0,4,8,12,16,20,24,27}_{en,zh,ar,hi,sw,ko}.pt\n     - Each file ~14042 samples × 3584 hidden dim × float32\n\n   - `/root/xling_calib_direction/results/transfer_results.csv`\n     - Contains ZS AUROC per (layer, language, method). Methods: zero_shot, mean_shift.\n     - 8 layers × 5 languages × 2 methods = 80 rows\n\n   - `/root/xling_calib_direction/results/train_metrics.csv` (copied from probes/)\n     - EN AUROC per layer: L0=0.624, L4=0.637, L8=0.635, L12=0.611, L16=0.669, L20=0.723, L24=0.749, L27=0.754\n\n   - `/root/xling_calib_direction/probes/` — 8 probe pkl files (probe_layer{N}.pkl) + train_metrics.csv\n\n   - `/root/xling_calib_direction/results_cka_mvp001/cka_matrix_qwen25_7b.csv`\n     - Pre-existing CKA matrix from an earlier partial run (CKA values only, no correlations/bootstrap)\n\n4. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01UY6LdVRieJBF7asKJkbG8V","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Let me try a different approach - instead of polling every few seconds, let me check every minute or so. But actually, the process has been running for 25.5 minutes. Let me estimate the total time more carefully.\n\nThe code does:\n1. Load all activations (fast, a few seconds)\n2. For each (layer, lang) pair: compute CKA with bootstrap\n3. Compute correlations (fast)\n4. Compute subspace angles (fast)\n5. Save results (fast)\n\nThere are 8 layers × 5 languages = 40 pairs. (hsic_xy / (hsic_xx.sqrt() * hsic_yy.sqrt())).item()\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef detect_layers(activations_dir: str) -> list[int]:\n45\t    \"\"\"Auto-detect available layer indices from CSV filename\")\n69\t    args = parser.parse_args()\n70\t\n71\t    if not os.path.isdir(args.activations_dir):\n72\t        print(f\"Error: activations_dir not found: {args.activations_dir}\", load_activations(args.activations_dir, layer, lang)\n92\t            n = min(ref_acts.shape[0], tgt_acts.shape[0])\n93\t            cka_val = linear_cka(ref_acts[:n], tgt_acts[:n])\n94\t Linear CKA (Centered Kernel Alignment) between cross-lingual activations.\n\nMeasures representational similarity between English and target language activations\nat each layer of an LLM, using parallel data (same samples in same order).\n\nInput:  activation files `layer{i}_{lang}.pt`, each containing\n        {\"activations\": Tensor(N, hidden_dim), \"labels\": Tensor(N,)}\nOutput: CSV with columns (layer, language, cka_value) + summary statistics.\n\nReference: Kornblith et al., \"Similarity of Neural Network Representations Revisited\",\n           ICML 2019. https://arxiv.org/abs/1905.00414\n\nRequires: torch\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport torch\n\n\ndef linear_cka(X: torch.Tensor, Y: torch.Tensor) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Linear CKA between two activation matrices.\n\n    Args:\n        X: (n, p) activations for language A\n        Y: (n, q) activations for language B\n        Both must share the same n (parallel samples in identical order).\n    \"\"\"\n    X = X - X.mean(0)\n    Y = Y - Y.mean(0)\n\n    hsic_xy = torch.norm(X.T @ Y, \"fro\") ** 2\n    hsic_xx = torch.norm(X.T @ X, \"fro\") ** 2\n    hsic_yy = torch.norm(Y.T @ Y, \"fro\") ** 2\n\n    return (hsic_xy / (hsic_xx.sqrt() * hsic_yy.sqrt())).item()\n\n\ndef detect_layers(activations_dir: str) -> list[int]:\n    \"\"\"Auto-detect available layer indices from filenames.\"\"\"\n    pattern = re.compile(r\"^layer(\\d+)_\\w+\\.pt$\")\n    layers = set()\n    for f in os.listdir(activations_dir):\n        m = pattern.match(f)\n        if m:\n            layers.add(int(m.group(1)))\n    return sorted(layers)\n\n\ndef load_activations(activations_dir: str, layer: int, lang: str) -> torch.Tensor:\n    path = os.path.join(activations_dir, f\"layer{layer}_{lang}.pt\")\n    data = torch.load(path, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=True)\n    return data[\"activations\"].float()\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Compute Linear CKA across languages and layers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--activations_dir\", default=\"./activations\", help=\"Directory with activation .pt files\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"./results\", help=\"Output directory\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--layers\", default=None, help=\"Comma-separated layer indices (default: auto-detect)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--languages\", nargs=\"+\", default=[\"zh\", \"ar\", \"hi\", \"sw\", \"ko\"], help=\"Target languages\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--reference_lang\", default=\"en\", help=\"Reference language\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_file\", default=\"cka_matrix.csv\", help=\"Output CSV filename\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    if not os.path.isdir(args.activations_dir):\n        print(f\"Error: activations_dir not found: {args.activations_dir}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    layers = [int(x) for x in args.layers.split(\",\")] if args.layers else detect_layers(args.activations_dir)\n    if not layers:\n        print(\"Error: no layers detected\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    output_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, args.output_file)\n\n    print(f\"Layers: {layers}\")\n    print(f\"Languages: {args.languages} (ref: {args.reference_lang})\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    for layer in layers:\n        ref_acts = load_activations(args.activations_dir, layer, args.reference_lang)\n        for lang in args.languages:\n            tgt_acts = load_activations(args.activations_dir, layer, lang)\n            n = min(ref_acts.shape[0], tgt_acts.shape[0])\n            cka_val = linear_cka(ref_acts[:n], tgt_acts[:n])\n            results.append((layer, lang, cka_val))\n            print(f\"  layer {layer:2d} | {lang} | CKA = {cka_val:.6f} (n={n})\")\n\n    # Write CSV\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(\"layer,language,cka_value\\n\")\n        for layer, lang, cka_val in results:\n            f.write(f\"{layer},{lang},{cka_val:.6f}\\n\")\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary\n    print(\"\\n=== Per-language mean CKA ===\")\n    for lang in args.languages:\n        vals = [c for _, l, c in results if l == lang]\n        print(f\"  {lang}: {sum(vals)/len(vals):.6f}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n=== Per-layer mean CKA ===\")\n    for layer in layers:\n        vals = [c for la, _, c in results if la == layer]\n        print(f\"  layer {layer:2d}: {sum(vals)/len(vals):.6f}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","numLines":118,"startLine":1,"totalLines":118}}}
